    {
        type: 'POST',
        url: 'https://www.sosyalbank.org/xmlhttp.php?action=prostats_reload',
        dataType: 'json',
        data: { ajax: 1, my_post_key: my_post_key },
        success: prostats_done
    });
	return false;
}

function prostats_done(data)
{
	if(data.hasOwnProperty("errors"))
	{
		$(".jGrowl").jGrowl("close");

		$.each(data.errors, function(i, message)
		{
			$.jGrowl(message);
		});
	}
	else if(data.html)
	{
		$("#prostats_table").html(data.html);
	}
}
-->
</script>
